# MW tier-A -- BODY-GROUNDED triage  (DRAFT; verify every kept case against the scan)
#
# The 1954 engine tier-A candidates were judged against MW's OWN entry text (csl-orig),
# not spelling alone. A spelling detector cannot tell a typo from a real word, an
# intentional variant / compounding form, or wrong-reading apparatus -- the entry can.
# Pipeline: deterministic body classification (triage_bodies.py) + a body-aware LLM
# pass over the 'realword' set + source confirmation of every TYPO verdict.
#
# FINDING: of 1954 tier-A candidates, 4 are body-confirmed fileable typos (0.2%).
# 1161 are real distinct words, 630 are spellings MW documents on purpose (w.r./v.l./
# in-comp./cross-ref -- filing them would CORRUPT MW), 11 are not in the current
# source, and 148 need eyes. 'Tier A' is high ENGINE confidence, NOT precision:
# do NOT bulk-apply it. Start with bucket 1; a human confirms each on the scan.

# ===== BUCKET 1  FILE-FIRST -- body-confirmed typo (verify on scan, then file) (4) =====

kattfna       -> kattfRa        [dcs=3 ndicts=1 body=realword x2]
    MW: kat—tfna  n. a fragrant grass, Suśr. || Pistia Stratiotes, L.
    judged: gloss 'fragrant grass, Pistia Stratiotes' = kat-tfRa; after f (vocalic r) n must be retroflex R; kattfna misspelled
    confirm: Entry 'kat-tfna n. a fragrant grass, Susr.; Pistia Stratiotes' = kat-tfRa; after vocalic f the n must be retroflex R (tfRa). Bare gloss, no v.l. Dental n is a misspelling.
    scan: http://www.sanskrit-lexicon.uni-koeln.de/scans/awork/apidev/servepdf.php?dict=MW&key=kattfna

Bawwaraka     -> BawwAraka      [dcs=3 ndicts=1 body=realword]
    MW: Bawwaraka  mf(ikA)n. venerable, L.
    judged: Gloss 'mf(ikA)n. venerable' is exactly bhaTTAraka's meaning; short-a Bawwaraka is the length-error variant.
    confirm: Entry 'Bawwaraka mf(ikA)n. venerable, L.' = bhaTTAraka 'venerable'; bare gloss, no v.l./See/cross-ref; next entry BawwAra confirms long-A family. Length error.
    scan: http://www.sanskrit-lexicon.uni-koeln.de/scans/awork/apidev/servepdf.php?dict=MW&key=Bawwaraka

akzAMsa       -> akzAMSa        [dcs=0 ndicts=2 body=realword]
    MW: akzAMsa  m. a degree of latitude.
    judged: 'a degree of latitude' = akza+aMSa 'degree/portion' (palatal S); dental-s akzAMsa is a sibilant misspelling; gloss fits the suggestion.
    confirm: Body 'akzA-Msa m. a degree of latitude' = akza+aMSa 'portion/degree' (palatal S); dental aMsa='shoulder' does not fit 'degree'. No v.l. Sibilant misspelling.
    scan: http://www.sanskrit-lexicon.uni-koeln.de/scans/awork/apidev/servepdf.php?dict=MW&key=akzAMsa

prativoDavya  -> prativoQavya   [dcs=0 ndicts=1 body=realword]
    MW: prati-˚voDavya  mfn. to be carried home, R.
    judged: Body 'to be carried home' = gerundive of vah = voQavya (retroflex Qh); dental D in suspect is the typo
    confirm: Body 'prati-voDavya mfn. to be carried home, R.' = gerundive of vah = voQavya (retroflex Qh by h+t sandhi); dental D form does not arise from vah. No v.l. Retroflex misspelling.
    scan: http://www.sanskrit-lexicon.uni-koeln.de/scans/awork/apidev/servepdf.php?dict=MW&key=prativoDavya

# ===== BUCKET 2  TYPO-UNSURE -- classified typo but source-confirm refuted (8) =====

SamASraya     -> samASraya      [dcs=3 ndicts=1 body=realword]
    MW: Sa—˚mASraya  m. the having recourse to a tr˚ life, ib.
    judged: gloss 'the having recourse to a ... life' = sam-ASraya 'recourse/refuge'; key's initial S is an s/S error
    confirm: k2 'Sa-mASraya', gloss 'having recourse to a tranquil(Sam) life'; next entry Samepsu confirms Sam- 'tranquil' prefix family. Initial palatal S is correct, not an error.
    scan: http://www.sanskrit-lexicon.uni-koeln.de/scans/awork/apidev/servepdf.php?dict=MW&key=SamASraya

sitaKaRqa     -> sitAKaRqa      [dcs=2 ndicts=2 body=realword]
    MW: sita—KaRqa  m. wh˚ lump-sugar, Hcat.; Dhūrtan.
    judged: gloss 'lump-sugar' fits sitA(sugar)+KaRqa = sitAKaRqa, not sita(white).
    confirm: Body 'sita-KaRqa m. white(wh) lump-sugar' — gloss explicitly 'white', fitting sita 'white'+KaRqa, NOT sitA 'sugar'. First member sita is correct.
    scan: http://www.sanskrit-lexicon.uni-koeln.de/scans/awork/apidev/servepdf.php?dict=MW&key=sitaKaRqa

piSitAsana    -> piSitASana     [dcs=2 ndicts=1 body=realword x2]
    MW: piSitAsana  m. a wolf, MBh. || piSitAsana  m. = prec. m., R.
    judged: body 'm. a wolf' = flesh-eater = piSita-aSana (palatal S); dental -asana 'seat' does not fit gloss, key misspelled
    confirm: Two consistent entries 'piSitA-sana m. a wolf' and '= prec.' both dental-s; cross-ref '= prec.' structure; intentional variant entry, not a one-off bare typo. Default false.
    scan: http://www.sanskrit-lexicon.uni-koeln.de/scans/awork/apidev/servepdf.php?dict=MW&key=piSitAsana

tryenI        -> tryeRI         [dcs=2 ndicts=2 body=realword]
    MW: try—e^nI  f. id., ŚBr. ii, 6, 4, 5.
    judged: body 'try-enI f. id., ŚBr.' refers to eRI 'antelope-doe' which is always retroflex R; dental tryenI is a typo for tryeRI
    confirm: Preceding entry tryeRI (retroflex R) already exists fully documented (KatySr/ApSr); tryenI 'id., SBr. ii,6,4,5' is a distinct attested SBr variant spelling, not a bare typo.
    scan: http://www.sanskrit-lexicon.uni-koeln.de/scans/awork/apidev/servepdf.php?dict=MW&key=tryenI

kaSikA        -> kASikA         [dcs=1 ndicts=2 body=realword]
    MW: kaSikA  f. a whip, Mṛcch.
    judged: body 'f. a whip, Mrcch.' = kaSA-derived kazikA; short ka- key is misspelling, but gloss is 'whip' not the city Kasika
    confirm: Body 'kaSikA f. a whip, Mrcch.' = kaSA-derived; gloss 'whip' fits short-a kaSikA, not the city kASikA (long A=different meaning). Short ka is correct.
    scan: http://www.sanskrit-lexicon.uni-koeln.de/scans/awork/apidev/servepdf.php?dict=MW&key=kaSikA

AtiSAyika     -> AtiSayika      [dcs=0 ndicts=2 body=realword]
    MW: AtiSAyika  mfn. expressing ascending gradation, Pat.
    judged: body 'expressing ascending gradation' fits taddhita of atiSaya = AtiSayika; second long A in SAy is anomalous
    confirm: Body 'AtiSAyika mfn. expressing ascending gradation, Pat.' = vrddhi taddhita of atiSaya; AtiSAyika is the regular vrddhi form. Long SAy is correct, not anomalous.
    scan: http://www.sanskrit-lexicon.uni-koeln.de/scans/awork/apidev/servepdf.php?dict=MW&key=AtiSAyika

gaqayitnu     -> gadayitnu      [dcs=0 ndicts=1 body=realword]
    MW: gaqayitnu  m. id., L. (cf. garday˚.)
    judged: Body 'id., L. (cf. garday˚.)' points to gada with dental d; retroflex q is the key typo for gadayitnu
    confirm: Coherent gaqa-family (retroflex q): gaqayanta 'a cloud', gaqaka, gaqera; 'id.'=cloud; body cross-refs '(cf. garday)'. Real distinct entry, retroflex q correct.
    scan: http://www.sanskrit-lexicon.uni-koeln.de/scans/awork/apidev/servepdf.php?dict=MW&key=gaqayitnu

nirasti       -> nirasTi        [dcs=2 ndicts=2 body=realword]
    MW: nir-˚asti  f. removal, destruction, Car.
    judged: body 'nir-asti f. removal, destruction, Car.' gloss 'removal' fits nir-asti not nirasTi; but prior-reason notes nis+asti reading, low confidence
    confirm: Body 'nir-asti f. removal, destruction, Car.'; gloss 'removal' fits nir-asti (the actual key), NOT the suggested nirasTi. Suggestion does not match the gloss.
    scan: http://www.sanskrit-lexicon.uni-koeln.de/scans/awork/apidev/servepdf.php?dict=MW&key=nirasti
